{"forms":[{"form":"atomic gardens","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A facility where plants are intentionally exposed to gamma radiation (often from a Cobalt-60 source) to induce beneficial mutations, creating new, improved crop varieties with desirable traits like disease resistance or higher yields, a practice used since the 1950s for developing new foods and materials; llants are arranged in concentric circles, with radiation levels decreasing further from the central source, leading to different effects: death near the source, severe abnormalities in the middle, and useful mutations (like better fruit or stress tolerance) in the outer rings."],"synonyms":[{"word":"gamma garden"}],"topics":["agriculture","business","horticulture","lifestyle"]}],"upstream_line":10538679,"upstream_offset":25445863218,"upstream_record_sha256":"0cf14392c78720bb45f3777a73d283112eab7c90772f2aaad3310721a61af88e","word":"atomic garden"}
